\begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced,breakable,boxrule=0.35pt,colback=VeryPaleBlue,colframe=AccentBlue!40,left=7pt,right=7pt,top=4pt,bottom=4pt,borderline west={2pt}{0pt}{AccentBlue},before skip=4pt,after skip=6pt]
\noindent {\bfseries 适用}：CET-6 为主、CET-4 基础补充｜{\bfseries 更新}：2026-10-29｜{\bfseries 格式}：Obsidian 兼容 Markdown\par

\noindent {\bfseries 核心方法}：不是背完整十年真题，而是依据近年已核查真题的{\bfseries 命题领域和叙述方式}，向现代中国的邻近主题扩展，建立可以迁移到新题目的词组、句法与段落能力。\par


\end{tcolorbox}

\begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced,breakable,boxrule=0.35pt,colback=VeryPaleBlue,colframe=AccentBlue!40,left=7pt,right=7pt,top=4pt,bottom=4pt,borderline west={2pt}{0pt}{AccentBlue},before skip=4pt,after skip=6pt]
{\bfseries\color{AccentBlue} 来源与适用范围}\par\vspace{1pt}
\noindent - {\bfseries 真题}：可直接与已核查的四六级翻译题材对应；{\bfseries 真题延伸}：由真题话题自然展开；{\bfseries 2026 拓展}：参考 2026 年公开政策与科技发展方向；{\bfseries 拓展}：合理储备的相邻话题。 - {\bfseries 主题标签不代表每条词组曾在真题出现，更不代表命中概率。}全部示例句和模拟翻译均为本资料另行编写。 - 指定 GitHub 仓库的文件夹当前无法逐个访问，所以不宣称对其中每套试题完成逐题统计。本资料通过公开可核查的原题页、历年题材目录和 2026 年政策文本构建。 - 英文以{\bfseries 准确、易写、可复用}为原则。政策术语有固定译法；普通表达可根据语境替换，绝不是唯一标准答案。\par


\end{tcolorbox}

\Needspace{6\baselineskip}
\begingroup\fontsize{10.5pt}{13pt}\selectfont\setlength{\parskip}{0pt}\tableofcontents\endgroup\vspace{4pt}
\section{使用指南：怎样把它变成翻译能力}

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2.0em,itemsep=1pt,parsep=0pt,topsep=2pt]
\item \noindent {\bfseries 优先看“历年主题 → 邻近延伸矩阵”}，理解出题角度；主题越新，不意味着命中率越高。\par


\item \noindent {\bfseries 每一专题先背关键词 8–12 个，再背动词搭配 5 个}；优先可以写出完整句子的表达。\par


\item \noindent {\bfseries 看两句迁移示例}：把主语换成另一领域，保持句法仍正确。\par


\item \noindent {\bfseries 学“句式工具箱”}，再做“先中文后答案”的翻译练习。\par


\item \noindent 考前只阅读 ZIP 中的《核心速背版》，不需要从头反复背整本。\par


\end{enumerate}

\begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced,breakable,boxrule=0.35pt,colback=VeryPaleGreen,colframe=TipGreen!40,left=7pt,right=7pt,top=4pt,bottom=4pt,borderline west={2pt}{0pt}{TipGreen},before skip=4pt,after skip=6pt]
{\bfseries\color{TipGreen} 重要原则}\par\vspace{1pt}
\noindent 对六级翻译而言，能准确写出 {\bfseries “事物/政策是什么 → 怎样应用/实施 → 有什么作用 → 仍存什么问题”}，比盲目背专业术语更有价值。不要为高级词牺牲语法正确与信息完整。\par


\end{tcolorbox}

\section{专题速查索引（按标题或编号快速定位）}

\noindent {\bfseries A · 中华文化、历史、地理与生活}：01 历史沿革与中国文化总述、02 节日习俗、家庭与礼仪、03 艺术、手工艺与非遗、04 饮食、茶文化与健康生活、05 地理、自然景观与旅游、06 古代工程、建筑与城市遗产、28 体育、传统养生与全民健身、29 历史人物、丝绸之路与文化交流、30 红色文化、革命历史与纪念场馆、31 文化产品出海、媒体与文旅融合、33 传统美德：尊老、节俭、勤劳与自强、34 中国语言、典籍与传统文学、35 古代科技与四大发明、36 竹、花卉与自然意象、37 服饰、婚礼、餐桌礼仪与赠礼、38 博物馆、数字文保与文化创意。\par

\noindent {\bfseries B · 现代科技、航天、交通与产业}：07 现代基础设施与综合交通、08 港口物流、供应链与贸易、09 航天、北斗与空间科学、10 人工智能、大数据与数字经济、11 无人机、低空经济与智能交通、14 智能制造、工业升级与机器人、15 新能源、电动汽车与能源转型、24 科研、创新与知识产权、39 未来通信、量子科技与数字基础设施、40 具身智能、机器人与人机协作、41 生物医药、脑机接口与科研伦理、42 无人机进阶：植保、配送、救援与空域、43 智能电网、储能与绿色氢能、44 海洋科技、海洋经济与海上工程。\par

\noindent {\bfseries C · 农业、乡村、医疗与数字生活}：12 智慧农业、粮食安全与乡村技术、13 医疗、远程医疗与公共卫生、26 移动互联网、支付与日常数字生活、45 农村电商、直播带货与县域物流、46 智慧农业进阶：数据、节水与病虫害防治、47 远程医疗进阶：基层诊疗与电子档案、48 公共卫生、疾病预防与健康生活、49 城市漫步、新文旅与服务消费。\par

\noindent {\bfseries D · 经济、政策、生态与社会治理}：16 生态文明、绿水青山与双碳、17 污染治理、循环经济与绿色城市、18 政策、治理与国家发展战略、19 经济、就业、消费与对外开放、20 区域协调、湾区与新型城镇化、21 乡村振兴、脱贫与共同富裕、50 生态修复、生物多样性与国家公园、51 政策表达进阶：新质生产力、开放与共同发展、52 数字治理、政务服务与数据安全、53 新型城镇化、城市更新与无障碍环境、54 生态文明进阶：循环经济与节约型社会。\par

\noindent {\bfseries E · 教育、民生、国际交流与未来方向}：22 养老、社会保障与公共服务、23 教育、人才培养与终身学习、25 国际合作、外交与全球治理、27 人口家庭、女性与社会变迁、32 防灾减灾、公共安全与韧性城市、55 创业、青年就业与数字技能、56 教育公平、人工智能教育与科研人才、57 老龄社会、银发经济与适老化服务、58 跨境电商、绿色贸易与国际合作、59 公共安全、极端天气与韧性基础设施。\par

\Needspace{6\baselineskip}
\section{A · 中华文化、历史、地理与生活}

\noindent 长期稳定的真题题材：文化、朝代、服饰、节日、自然地理、礼仪。\par

\Needspace{22\baselineskip}
\subsection{01 历史沿革与中国文化总述 {\color{AccentBlue} [真题/相近真题]}}

\begingroup
\fontsize{10.5pt}{12.85pt}\selectfont
\setlength{\tabcolsep}{2.5pt}
\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.04}
\arrayrulecolor{RuleGray}
\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{6.742cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{11.238cm}@{}}

中华文明 & Chinese civilization \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}

\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}

\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}

\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}

\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}

\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
民族精神 & national spirit \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
价值观念 & values and beliefs \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}

\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
受到广泛欢迎 & enjoy widespread popularity \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
影响深远 & have a far-reaching influence \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
以……而闻名 & be known for … \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
被认为是…… & be regarded as … \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
逐渐演变为…… & gradually evolve into … \\
\end{longtable}

\vspace{-2pt}

\noindent {\bfseries 迁移句训练（示例均为原创）}\par

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=1.7em,itemsep=1pt,parsep=0pt,topsep=2pt]
\item \noindent 中文：中华传统文化经过长期发展，形成了丰富多样的艺术表现形式。\\ 英文：Traditional Chinese culture has developed over a long period and given rise to a wide variety of artistic forms.\par


\item \noindent 中文：这项习俗不仅具有历史价值，也反映了人们对家庭的重视。\\ 英文：This custom is valuable not only for its history but also for what it reveals about the importance of family.\par


\end{itemize}

\begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced,breakable,boxrule=0.35pt,colback=VeryPaleRose,colframe=WarmRed!40,left=7pt,right=7pt,top=4pt,bottom=4pt,borderline west={2pt}{0pt}{WarmRed},before skip=4pt,after skip=6pt]
{\bfseries\color{WarmRed} 易混术语}\par\vspace{1pt}
\noindent {\bfseries 具身智能}是 {\itshape embodied intelligence}；{\bfseries 人形机器人}是 {\itshape humanoid robots}，两者不是完全同义词。\par

\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
实现绿色增长 & achieve green growth \\
\end{longtable}
\endgroup
\vspace{-2pt}

\noindent {\bfseries 迁移句训练（示例均为原创）}\par

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=1.7em,itemsep=1pt,parsep=0pt,topsep=2pt]
\item \noindent 中文：绿水青山就是金山银山，生态保护能够带来长期效益。\\ 英文：Lucid waters and lush mountains are invaluable assets, and ecological conservation can bring long-term benefits.\par


\item \noindent 中文：绿色发展强调经济活动与环境保护相协调。\\ 英文：Green development seeks to align economic activities with environmental protection.\par


\end{itemize}

\begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced,breakable,boxrule=0.35pt,colback=VeryPaleRose,colframe=WarmRed!40,left=7pt,right=7pt,top=4pt,bottom=4pt,borderline west={2pt}{0pt}{WarmRed},before skip=4pt,after skip=6pt]
{\bfseries\color{WarmRed} 易混术语}\par\vspace{1pt}
\noindent {\bfseries 绿水青山就是金山银山}的常用官方化表述是 {\itshape Lucid waters and lush mountains are invaluable assets}；普通景观描写不要机械套政治口号。\par


\end{tcolorbox}

\begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced,breakable,boxrule=0.35pt,colback=VeryPaleGreen,colframe=TipGreen!40,left=7pt,right=7pt,top=4pt,bottom=4pt,borderline west={2pt}{0pt}{TipGreen},before skip=4pt,after skip=6pt]
{\bfseries\color{TipGreen} 易混辨析}\par\vspace{1pt}
\noindent {\color{AccentBlue} improve} 后常接 {\bfseries quality, efficiency, conditions}；{\color{AccentBlue} increase} 后常接 {\bfseries income, output, number}；{\color{AccentBlue} enhance} 常接 {\bfseries awareness, competitiveness, capabilities}。{\color{AccentBlue} provide convenience} 在不少语境不如 {\bfseries make … more convenient / offer convenient services} 自然。{\color{AccentBlue} develop} 是“发展/研发”，{\color{AccentBlue} exploit} 不是“开发”的通用替换词。\par


\end{tcolorbox}

P01 & [事物] dates back to [时期]. & Chinese calligraphy dates back thousands of years. & 中国书法可以追溯到数千年前。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P02 & [事物] originated in [地点] and later spread to [地区]. & Tea culture originated in China and later spread to many other countries. & 茶文化起源于中国，后来传播至许多其他国家。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P03 & Over the centuries, [事物] has gradually developed into [形态]. & Over the centuries, the festival has developed into an important family occasion. & 数百年来，这一节日逐渐成为重要的家庭节日。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P04 & [事物], which has a history of [时间], is known for [特征]. & The bridge, which has a history of more than 1,000 years, is known for its ingenious design. & 这座有一千多年历史的桥以精巧设计闻名。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P05 & Traditionally, people would [动作] to [目的]. & Traditionally, families would gather to celebrate the festival. & 按照传统，家人会团聚庆祝节日。 \\
\end{longtable}
\endgroup
\vspace{-2pt}

\Needspace{12\baselineskip}
\subsection{描述—地点、规模与结构}

\fontsize{10.5pt}{12.85pt}\selectfont

\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.04}

P06 & Located in [地点], [主体] is one of [范围] … & Located in Shanghai, the port is one of the major transport hubs in the region. & 该港口位于上海，是该地区的主要交通枢纽之一。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P07 & [主体] covers an area of [数字] and consists of [部分]. & The park covers a large area and consists of several scenic zones. & 该公园占地广阔，由几个景区组成。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P08 & [主体] is equipped with [设施] and can [能力]. & The hospital is equipped with modern equipment and can provide remote consultations. & 该医院配备现代设备，可开展远程会诊。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P09 & With [特色], [主体] attracts [对象]. & With its unique scenery, the region attracts tourists from around the world. & 凭借独特风景，该地区吸引世界各地游客。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P10 & [主体] serves as an important [身份] connecting [A] with [B]. & The bridge serves as an important transport link connecting the two cities. & 这座大桥是连接两座城市的重要交通纽带。 \\
\end{longtable}
\endgroup
\vspace{-2pt}

\subsection{发展—变化与趋势}

\begingroup
\fontsize{10.5pt}{12.85pt}\selectfont
\setlength{\tabcolsep}{2.5pt}
\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.04}
\arrayrulecolor{RuleGray}
\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{1.687cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{4.618cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{7.548cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{3.907cm}@{}}

\rowcolor{HeadBlue} {\bfseries 编号} & {\bfseries 可迁移骨架} & {\bfseries 自编英语示例} & {\bfseries 对应中文} \\

\bottomrule
\endfoot
P11 & In recent years, [领域] has made remarkable progress in [方面]. & In recent years, China has made remarkable progress in space technology. & 近年来，中国在航天技术方面取得显著进展。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P12 & With the development of [技术], [现象] has become increasingly common. & With the development of digital technology, online consultations have become increasingly common. & 随着数字技术的发展，在线问诊越来越普遍。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P13 & As [条件] improves, more people are able to [行为]. & As transport conditions improve, more people are able to travel conveniently. & 随着交通条件改善，更多人可以便捷出行。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P14 & [主体] has undergone great changes over the past [时间]. & Rural communities have undergone great changes over the past decade. & 过去十年农村社区发生巨大变化。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P15 & [主体] is playing an increasingly important role in [领域]. & Renewable energy is playing an increasingly important role in electricity generation. & 可再生能源在发电中发挥越来越重要的作用。 \\
\end{longtable}
\endgroup
\vspace{-2pt}

\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.04}

\bottomrule

P16 & [主体] has independently developed [设备/系统] to [目的]. & Researchers have independently developed a system to improve crop monitoring. & 科研人员自主研发了一套系统用于改善农作物监测。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P17 & [技术] is widely used in [领域A], [领域B] and [领域C]. & Satellite navigation is widely used in transport, agriculture and disaster response. & 卫星导航广泛应用于交通、农业和灾害应对。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P18 & By using [技术], [主体] can [结果]. & By using drones, farmers can monitor large areas of farmland more efficiently. & 利用无人机，农民可以更高效地监测大面积农田。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P19 & The application of [技术] has made it possible to [行为]. & The application of telemedicine has made it possible to consult doctors remotely. & 远程医疗的应用使远程咨询医生成为可能。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P20 & [技术] enables [对象] to [动作] without [限制]. & Digital platforms enable patients to make appointments without visiting a hospital. & 数字平台使患者无需到医院即可预约。 \\
\end{longtable}

P21 & The government has introduced a series of measures to [目标]. & The government has introduced a series of measures to improve healthcare services. & 政府采取了一系列措施改善医疗服务。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P22 & To [目标], [主体] has stepped up efforts to [行动]. & To protect the environment, cities have stepped up efforts to reduce emissions. & 为了保护环境，各城市加大了减排力度。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}

P24 & A range of policies have been implemented to support [群体/领域]. & A range of policies have been implemented to support rural development. & 一系列政策已落实以支持乡村发展。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P25 & [目标] is an important part of [战略/规划]. & Developing public transport is an important part of urban planning. & 发展公共交通是城市规划的重要组成部分。 \\

\Needspace{12\baselineskip}
\subsection{意义—贡献、影响与成效}

\begingroup
\fontsize{10.5pt}{12.85pt}\selectfont
\setlength{\tabcolsep}{2.5pt}
\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.04}
\arrayrulecolor{RuleGray}
\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{1.687cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{4.618cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{7.548cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{3.907cm}@{}}
\toprule

\midrule
\endfirsthead
\rowcolor{HeadBlue} {\bfseries 编号} & {\bfseries 可迁移骨架} & {\bfseries 自编英语示例} & {\bfseries 对应中文} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endfoot
P26 & [主体] has made a significant contribution to [领域]. & Hybrid rice has made a significant contribution to global food security. & 杂交水稻为全球粮食安全作出重要贡献。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P27 & [措施] not only [结果A] but also [结果B]. & The project not only improves connectivity but also creates job opportunities. & 该项目不仅改善互联互通，还创造就业机会。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P28 & [行动] helps to [结果A] while [结果B]. & Smart irrigation helps to save water while improving crop yields. & 智能灌溉有助于节水，同时提高产量。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P29 & [成果] marks an important step forward in [领域]. & The launch marks an important step forward in space exploration. & 此次发射标志着太空探索迈出重要一步。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P30 & [行为] benefits both [A] and [B]. & Green transport benefits both residents and the environment. & 绿色交通既有利于居民，也有利于环境。 \\
\end{longtable}
\endgroup
\vspace{-2pt}

\endhead
\bottomrule
\endfoot
P31 & Thanks to [有利因素], [结果]. & Thanks to improved infrastructure, travel has become much easier. & 得益于基础设施改善，出行更加方便。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P32 & As a result of [因素], [结果]. & As a result of technological advances, production costs have fallen. & 技术进步使生产成本下降。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P33 & [原因], thereby [结果]. & The system collects real-time data, thereby improving decision-making. & 该系统收集实时数据，从而改善决策。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P34 & [行动] aims to [目标] by [方法]. & The program aims to improve healthcare access by expanding online services. & 该计划旨在通过扩展在线服务提高医疗可及性。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P35 & This is largely because [原因]. & This is largely because digital tools make information easier to access. & 这主要因为数字工具让信息更容易获取。 \\
\end{longtable}
\endgroup

P36 & Compared with [过去/对象], [主体] is now [变化]. & Compared with the past, public transport is now much more convenient. & 与过去相比，公共交通如今便捷得多。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P37 & While [情况A], [情况B]. & While traditional methods remain valuable, new technologies offer additional options. & 传统方法仍有价值，但新技术提供了更多选择。 \\

\end{longtable}

\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{1.687cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{4.618cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{7.548cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{3.907cm}@{}}

\rowcolor{HeadBlue} {\bfseries 编号} & {\bfseries 可迁移骨架} & {\bfseries 自编英语示例} & {\bfseries 对应中文} \\

\bottomrule
\endfoot
P41 & [名词], which [附加说明], [主句]. & The satellite, which provides positioning services, is used in many fields. & 这颗提供定位服务的卫星应用于许多领域。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P42 & [名词] designed to [目的] … & A system designed to monitor crops can reduce labor costs. & 一套专为监测作物设计的系统能够降低人工成本。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P43 & [名词] located in [地点] … & The museum located in the city center attracts many visitors. & 位于市中心的博物馆吸引大量游客。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P44 & [名词] featuring [特征] … & A factory featuring automated production lines can operate efficiently. & 拥有自动化生产线的工厂可以高效运行。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P45 & It is a place where [从句]. & It is a place where visitors can experience traditional art. & 这是游客可以体验传统艺术的地方。 \\

P51 & The number of [名词复数] has increased from [A] to [B]. & The number of charging stations has increased from 100 to 300. & 充电站数量从100个增至300个。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P52 & [数值] accounts for [百分比] of [总体]. & Public transport accounts for 40\% of daily journeys in this example. & 在本例中公共交通占每日出行的40\%。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P53 & [指标] increased by [百分比] compared with [基准]. & Crop yields increased by 20\% compared with the previous year. & 农作物产量比上年提高了20\%。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P54 & [主体] is approximately [倍数] as large as [对象]. & The new area is approximately twice as large as the old one. & 新区域面积约为旧区域的两倍。 \\

P55 & By [年份], [主体] is expected to [结果]. & By 2035, the region is expected to develop further. & 预计到2035年，该地区将进一步发展。 \\

\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{1.687cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{4.618cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{7.548cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{3.907cm}@{}}

\rowcolor{HeadBlue} {\bfseries 编号} & {\bfseries 可迁移骨架} & {\bfseries 自编英语示例} & {\bfseries 对应中文} \\

\bottomrule
\endfoot
P56 & Although [困难], [主体] has [成就]. & Although the work was challenging, the team made substantial progress. & 尽管工作艰巨，团队仍取得实质进展。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P57 & Despite [困难名词], [主体] continues to [行动]. & Despite technical difficulties, researchers continue to improve the system. & 尽管存在技术困难，研究人员仍持续改进系统。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}

\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}

P60 & With continued investment, [愿景] may become possible. & With continued investment, wider access to medical services may become possible. & 随着持续投入，更普遍的医疗服务可及性有望实现。 \\

\subsection{四级→六级升级：避免炫技}

\begingroup
\fontsize{10.5pt}{12.85pt}\selectfont
\setlength{\tabcolsep}{2.5pt}
\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.04}
\arrayrulecolor{RuleGray}
\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{1.687cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{4.618cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{7.548cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{3.907cm}@{}}
\toprule
\rowcolor{HeadBlue} {\bfseries 编号} & {\bfseries 可迁移骨架} & {\bfseries 自编英语示例} & {\bfseries 对应中文} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\rowcolor{HeadBlue} {\bfseries 编号} & {\bfseries 可迁移骨架} & {\bfseries 自编英语示例} & {\bfseries 对应中文} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endfoot
P61 & There is/are … → [对象] has seen … & The village has seen major improvements in public services. & 该村的公共服务有了显著改善。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P62 & People can … → [技术] enables people to … & Telemedicine enables people to receive medical advice remotely. & 远程医疗使人们能够远程获得医疗建议。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P63 & It is important … → [事物] plays a crucial role in … & Infrastructure plays a crucial role in regional development. & 基础设施在区域发展中发挥关键作用。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P64 & [主体] is useful … → [主体] contributes to … & Smart agriculture contributes to more efficient food production. & 智慧农业有助于更高效的粮食生产。 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
P65 & Many people like … → [事物] has gained popularity among … & Low-carbon travel has gained popularity among urban residents. & 低碳出行在城市居民中日益普及。 \\
\end{longtable}
\endgroup
\vspace{-2pt}

\Needspace{6\baselineskip}
\section{六、30 个高频易错点}

\begingroup
\fontsize{10.5pt}{12.85pt}\selectfont
\setlength{\tabcolsep}{2.5pt}
\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.04}
\arrayrulecolor{RuleGray}
\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{4.825cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{5.182cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{7.863cm}@{}}
\toprule
\rowcolor{HeadBlue} {\bfseries 易错处} & {\bfseries 可能出现的问题} & {\bfseries 推荐处理} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\rowcolor{HeadBlue} {\bfseries 易错处} & {\bfseries 可能出现的问题} & {\bfseries 推荐处理} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endfoot
1. “随着……” & {\color{AccentBlue} With ... develops} 主谓不完整 & 用 {\bfseries As … develops} 或 {\bfseries With the development of …} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
2. “促进发展” & 逐字堆成 {\color{AccentBlue} promote the development of development} & {\bfseries promote development} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
3. “取得成就” & {\color{AccentBlue} get a big success} 口语生硬 & {\bfseries achieve success / make remarkable progress} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
4. “人们的生活水平” & {\color{AccentBlue} people's life level} & {\bfseries people’s living standards} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
5. “提高生活质量” & {\color{AccentBlue} raise life quality} & {\bfseries improve quality of life} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
6. “基础设施” & {\color{AccentBlue} basic facilities} 不总对应 & {\bfseries infrastructure}；具体场所可用 {\bfseries public facilities} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
7. “公共设施” & 与基础设施完全混同 & {\bfseries public facilities / public amenities}；铁路路桥属 {\bfseries infrastructure} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
8. “发展迅速” & {\color{AccentBlue} develops fastly}（fastly 不是规范副词） & {\bfseries develop rapidly / grow quickly} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
9. “近年来” & {\color{AccentBlue} in recently years} & {\bfseries in recent years} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
10. “众所周知” & 处处添加 {\color{AccentBlue} As we all know} & 原文无此信息就不必添加 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
11. “越来越多” & {\color{AccentBlue} more and more increasing} & {\bfseries a growing number of …} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
12. “广泛应用” & {\color{AccentBlue} be widely using} & {\bfseries be widely used / applied} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
13. “对……起作用” & {\color{AccentBlue} play an important role to} & {\bfseries play an important role in …} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
14. “做贡献” & {\color{AccentBlue} make contribution for} & {\bfseries make a contribution to …} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
15. “受欢迎” & {\color{AccentBlue} be welcomed by people} 滥用 & {\bfseries be popular with / enjoy popularity among} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
16. “传统文化” & {\color{AccentBlue} tradition culture} & {\bfseries traditional culture} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
17. “非物质文化遗产” & {\color{AccentBlue} non-material cultural legacy} & {\bfseries intangible cultural heritage} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
18. “绿水青山就是金山银山” & 按颜色与矿山逐字硬译 & 固定政策英语：{\bfseries lucid waters and lush mountains are invaluable assets} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
19. “双碳目标” & {\color{AccentBlue} double carbon goals} & {\bfseries goals of carbon peaking and carbon neutrality} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
20. “碳达峰” & {\color{AccentBlue} carbon top} & {\bfseries carbon peaking / peak carbon emissions} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
21. “无人机” & {\color{AccentBlue} unpeople plane} & {\bfseries drone / unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
22. “远程医疗” & {\color{AccentBlue} long-distance hospital} & {\bfseries telemedicine / remote medical services} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
23. “智慧农业” & {\color{AccentBlue} wise farming} & {\bfseries smart agriculture / precision agriculture}（侧重不同） \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
24. “新质生产力” & {\color{AccentBlue} new productive quality} & {\bfseries new quality productive forces} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
25. “交通方便” & {\color{AccentBlue} the traffic is convenient} 万能化 & {\bfseries transport is convenient / it is easy to get around} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
26. “有着悠久历史” & {\color{AccentBlue} has long history} & {\bfseries has a long history} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
27. “很多种” & {\color{AccentBlue} many kinds of different} 重复 & {\bfseries various / a wide range of} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
28. 现在完成时 & {\color{AccentBlue} since 2010, it develops} & {\bfseries Since 2010, it has developed …} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
29. 不仅……而且 & {\color{AccentBlue} not only ... but also ...} 不平行 & {\bfseries not only improves A but also benefits B}（保持同类结构） \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
30. 数据翻译 & {\color{AccentBlue} increased to 20\%} 与 {\color{AccentBlue} by 20\%} 混用 & {\bfseries to} = 达到某数；{\bfseries by} = 增量；{\bfseries from A to B} = 起止值 \\
\end{longtable}
\endgroup
\vspace{-2pt}

\begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced,breakable,boxrule=0.35pt,colback=VeryPaleRose,colframe=WarmRed!40,left=7pt,right=7pt,top=4pt,bottom=4pt,borderline west={2pt}{0pt}{WarmRed},before skip=4pt,after skip=6pt]
{\bfseries\color{WarmRed} 四个容易损失准确性的高级细节}\par\vspace{1pt}
\noindent （1） {\bfseries increase by 30\%} 表示“增长 30\%”，{\bfseries increase to 30\%} 表示“增加到 30\%”；（2） {\bfseries twice as large as} 是“两倍大”，避免不清晰的 {\itshape twice larger than}；（3） {\bfseries research} 在“科研”义下通常不可数，写 {\bfseries conduct research}，而不是 {\itshape do many researches}；（4） {\bfseries equipment、information、infrastructure} 通常不可数，不能随意加 {\color{AccentBlue} -s}。\par


\end{tcolorbox}

\Needspace{6\baselineskip}
\section{七、句间逻辑：10 组一眼可复用的连接方式}

\begingroup
\fontsize{10.5pt}{12.85pt}\selectfont
\setlength{\tabcolsep}{2.5pt}
\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.04}
\arrayrulecolor{RuleGray}
\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{4.825cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{5.182cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{7.863cm}@{}}
\toprule
\rowcolor{HeadBlue} {\bfseries 关系} & {\bfseries 表达} & {\bfseries 注意} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\rowcolor{HeadBlue} {\bfseries 关系} & {\bfseries 表达} & {\bfseries 注意} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endfoot
时间起点 & since then / since its establishment & 后接现在完成时常见 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
背景变化 & with the rise of … / as … develops & 区分介词和从句 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
原因 & because of / due to / thanks to & thanks to 更适合积极原因 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
结果 & therefore / as a result / consequently & 不要机械叠加两个结果词 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
目的 & in order to / with the aim of …ing & 目的状语与主语逻辑相符 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
递进 & in addition / moreover / furthermore & 不要每句都用复杂连接词 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
对比 & by contrast / compared with / whereas & whereas 后接从句 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
并列 & not only … but also … / both … and … & 语法平行 \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
让步 & although / despite / even though & despite 后接名词或 {\color{AccentBlue} -ing} \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
举例 & for example / such as / including & such as 后通常接名词短语 \\
\end{longtable}
\endgroup
\vspace{-2pt}

\Needspace{6\baselineskip}
\section{八、翻译流程与限时策略}

\begin{enumerate}[leftmargin=2.0em,itemsep=1pt,parsep=0pt,topsep=2pt]
\item \noindent {\bfseries 60 秒提取主干}：每句先标出“谁做什么”，再找时间、地点、原因、结果。\par


\item \noindent {\bfseries 3–5 分钟规划术语}：挑出专有名词；先写熟悉、确定的表达，不对生词死磕。\par


\item \noindent {\bfseries 15–18 分钟成段翻译}：优先短而正确的句子；长句仅在关系明确时使用。\par


\item \noindent {\bfseries 3–5 分钟检查}：主谓一致、时态、冠词、单复数、介词、拼写、专名、数字。\par


\item \noindent {\bfseries 最后 1 分钟回查信息完整性}：尤其不要漏掉“过去—现在”“原因—结果”“用途—作用”“数量—占比”。\par


\end{enumerate}

\begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced,breakable,boxrule=0.35pt,colback=VeryPaleBlue,colframe=AccentBlue!40,left=7pt,right=7pt,top=4pt,bottom=4pt,borderline west={2pt}{0pt}{AccentBlue},before skip=4pt,after skip=6pt]
{\bfseries\color{AccentBlue} 三层能力优先级}\par\vspace{1pt}
\noindent {\bfseries 第一层：信息准确无遗漏}；{\bfseries 第二层：固定搭配和时态正确}；{\bfseries 第三层：定语从句、分词结构和逻辑衔接}。难词不等于高分；如果不确定高级词，使用准确的普通词更稳妥。\par


\end{tcolorbox}

\Needspace{6\baselineskip}
\section{九、补充 25 个跨题材强化句式（V2 新增）}

\begingroup
\fontsize{10.5pt}{12.85pt}\selectfont
\setlength{\tabcolsep}{2.5pt}
\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.04}
\arrayrulecolor{RuleGray}
\begin{longtable}{@{}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{4.825cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{5.182cm}>{\RaggedRight\arraybackslash}p{7.863cm}@{}}
\toprule
\rowcolor{HeadBlue} {\bfseries 句式/结构} & {\bfseries 使用场景} & {\bfseries 原创示例} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
\rowcolor{HeadBlue} {\bfseries 句式/结构} & {\bfseries 使用场景} & {\bfseries 原创示例} \\
\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule
\endfoot
make it easier for sb. to do sth. & 科技带来便利 & Telemedicine makes it easier for rural patients to consult specialists. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
allow sb. to do sth. remotely & 远程操作 & The system allows operators to monitor equipment remotely. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
give sb. access to sth. & 服务可及 & Online learning gives students access to more courses. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
reduce the need for … & 减少资源成本 & Drones reduce the need for manual inspection. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
make full use of … & 充分利用 & Farmers can make full use of weather data. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
be designed to do sth. & 设计用途 & The service is designed to help older residents. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
take measures to do sth. & 政策措施 & Local authorities have taken measures to improve public transport. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
take account of … & 考虑条件 & City planning should take account of residents’ needs. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
make … more accessible to … & 普惠受益 & Digital services make information more accessible to the public. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
help bridge the gap between A and B & 缩小差距 & Better internet access helps bridge the gap between urban and rural areas. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
ensure that … & 保障目标 & Regulations are needed to ensure that drones operate safely. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
strike a balance between A and B & 平衡两方 & Cities should strike a balance between development and conservation. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
address the problem of … & 解决问题 & The initiative seeks to address the problem of food waste. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
lead to / result in … & 结果 & Better logistics can lead to lower delivery costs. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
be attributed to … & 归因 & The improvement can be partly attributed to technological advances. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
In addition to …, … & 补充方面 & In addition to reducing costs, the system saves time. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
What matters is … & 核心因素 & What matters is whether the technology meets real needs. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
This is especially important for … & 受益人群 & This is especially important for patients in remote areas. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
… rather than … & 纠正理解 & The program focuses on prevention rather than treatment alone. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
… ranging from A to B & 领域举例 & The technology is used in areas ranging from farming to healthcare. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
both … and … & 并列效果 & The project benefits both local businesses and residents. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
make progress in doing sth. & 发展变化 & Researchers have made progress in developing new crop varieties. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
continue to do sth. & 延续发展 & The city continues to expand its public service network. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
be increasingly integrated into … & 技术融入 & AI is increasingly integrated into manufacturing processes. \\
\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}
provide a foundation for … & 奠定基础 & Better infrastructure provides a foundation for regional growth. \\
\end{longtable}
\endgroup
\vspace{-2pt}

\Needspace{12\baselineskip}
\subsection{6 种中文句式的拆译示范（最值得练）}

\noindent {\bfseries 示范 1：中文无主句（“近年来不断推进……”）}\\ 中文：近年来，不断推进农村数字基础设施建设。\\ 译法：{\itshape In recent years, greater efforts have been made to improve digital infrastructure in rural areas.}\\ 点评：原句没有明示行动者，可以用被动结构，避免凭空加入主语。\par

\noindent {\bfseries 示范 2：动词连用（“通过……提高……减少……”）}\\ 中文：通过使用传感器监测土壤状况，提高灌溉效率，减少水资源浪费。\\ 译法：{\itshape By monitoring soil conditions with sensors, farmers can make irrigation more efficient and reduce water waste.}\\ 点评：补出合理的执行者 farmers，使英语主句结构完整。\par

\noindent {\bfseries 示范 3：长定语前置（“位于……的……系统”）}\\ 中文：位于偏远山区的医院使用的新系统能够连接大城市的专家。\\ 译法：{\itshape The new system used by hospitals in remote mountain areas can connect them with specialists in major cities.}\\ 点评：用后置 {\itshape used by …} 处理修饰内容，避免把所有信息塞在名词前面。\par

\noindent {\bfseries 示范 4：抽象名词动词化（“……的实现”）}\\ 中文：高精度定位的实现极大改善了交通管理。\\ 译法：{\itshape The ability to provide highly accurate positioning has greatly improved traffic management.}\\ 点评：不必机械翻译“实现”为 {\itshape realization}。\par

\noindent {\bfseries 示范 5：政策目的句（“为……，出台……”）}\\ 中文：为改善基层医疗服务，政府采取了一系列措施。\\ 译法：{\itshape To improve primary healthcare, the government has introduced a series of measures.}\\ 点评：目的不等于结果，避免用 {\itshape because}。\par

\noindent {\bfseries 示范 6：褒义口号的客观转述}\\ 中文：绿水青山就是金山银山，保护生态环境有助于推动可持续发展。\\ 译法：{\itshape Lucid waters and lush mountains are invaluable assets. Protecting the environment helps promote sustainable development.}\\ 点评：按原义翻译，不能凭空添加未经原文说明的成效数字。\par

\Needspace{12\baselineskip}
\subsection{五组“不是一个意思”的重要辨析}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=1.7em,itemsep=1pt,parsep=0pt,topsep=2pt]
\item \noindent {\bfseries 应用}：apply technology {\bfseries to} a task / a technology {\bfseries is used in} agriculture。\par


\item \noindent {\bfseries 普及}：the {\bfseries widespread use/adoption} of a technology；不是任何场合都用 {\itshape popularize}。\par


\item \noindent {\bfseries 保障}：{\bfseries ensure} food security；{\bfseries protect} personal data；{\bfseries safeguard} the rights of older people，搭配不同。\par


\item \noindent {\bfseries 政策}：{\bfseries issue/introduce} policies；{\bfseries implement} measures；{\bfseries achieve} targets。政策“出台”和“落实”不同。\par


\item \noindent {\bfseries 数字}：{\bfseries increase by 20\%}（增幅）≠ {\bfseries increase to 20\%}（终值）≠ {\bfseries account for 20\%}（比例）。\par


\end{itemize}

\Needspace{6\baselineskip}
\section{十、24 句跨领域基础精练（先做再看译文）}

\Needspace{6\baselineskip}
\section{十一、10 篇跨题材基础模拟翻译（综合实战）}

\begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced,breakable,boxrule=0.35pt,colback=VeryPaleRose,colframe=WarmRed!40,left=7pt,right=7pt,top=4pt,bottom=4pt,borderline west={2pt}{0pt}{WarmRed},before skip=4pt,after skip=6pt]
{\bfseries\color{WarmRed} 明确区分来源}\par\vspace{1pt}
\noindent 以下 {\bfseries 10 篇全部为原创模拟材料}，没有直接复制考试原文，不标称“押题命中”。各篇约 140–200 汉字，目的是把前面的短语、句式组合成实际翻译能力。\par


\end{tcolorbox}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{综合练习 01 · A 智慧农业与粮食安全}

\noindent {\bfseries 中文题目}\par

\noindent 近年来，随着数字技术的发展，智慧农业正在改变传统农业生产方式。农民可以利用无人机监测农田，利用传感器获取土壤和天气数据，并根据实际情况安排灌溉。这些技术不仅提高了农业生产效率，还减少了水资源和化肥的浪费。与此同时，科研人员不断培育抗旱、抗病的新品种，为保障粮食安全和推动农业可持续发展提供支持。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文（先独立完成）}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent In recent years, with the development of digital technology, smart agriculture has been changing traditional farming practices. Farmers can use drones to monitor farmland, collect soil and weather data through sensors, and plan irrigation according to actual conditions. These technologies not only improve agricultural productivity but also reduce the waste of water and fertilizers. Meanwhile, researchers are developing new crop varieties that are more resistant to drought and disease, helping ensure food security and promote sustainable agriculture.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{综合练习 02 · B 远程医疗与城乡医疗服务}

\noindent {\bfseries 中文题目}\par

\noindent 中国一些地区正在积极发展远程医疗，以改善基层医疗服务。借助互联网平台，偏远地区的患者可以与大城市的医生进行在线会诊，减少长途奔波。电子病历和人工智能辅助诊断也在逐步应用于医疗实践。不过，发展智慧医疗不仅需要先进设备，还需要保护患者隐私、培训医护人员，并不断完善相关公共服务。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文（先独立完成）}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent Some regions in China are actively developing telemedicine to improve primary healthcare services. Through online platforms, patients in remote areas can consult doctors in major cities without making long journeys. Electronic medical records and AI-assisted diagnosis are also gradually being used in medical practice. However, developing smart healthcare requires not only advanced equipment but also the protection of patient privacy, training for medical staff, and continuous improvements in public services.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{综合练习 03 · C 无人机与低空经济}

\noindent {\bfseries 中文题目}\par

\noindent 无人机的应用早已不限于航拍。如今，无人机还被用于农业植保、物流配送、灾害监测和应急救援。在一些山区，无人机可以快速运送急需物资，帮助人们克服交通不便的困难。随着相关技术不断成熟，低空经济有望创造新的就业机会，但飞行安全、空域管理和隐私保护仍是需要认真解决的问题。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文（先独立完成）}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent The use of drones is no longer limited to aerial photography. Today, they are also used for crop protection, deliveries, disaster monitoring and emergency rescue. In some mountainous areas, drones can quickly deliver urgently needed supplies, helping people overcome difficulties caused by poor transport links. As the technology continues to mature, the low-altitude economy may create new job opportunities. However, flight safety, airspace management and privacy protection remain important challenges.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{综合练习 04 · D 绿水青山与城市低碳发展}

\noindent {\bfseries 中文题目}\par

\noindent 绿水青山就是金山银山。越来越多的城市认识到，保护生态环境与提高居民生活质量密切相关。为减少污染和碳排放，一些城市正在完善公共交通系统，增加城市绿地，并推广垃圾分类和绿色建筑。绿色发展不仅能改善自然环境，也能创造新的产业机会。然而，实现长期目标仍需要政府、企业和公众的共同努力。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文（先独立完成）}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent Lucid waters and lush mountains are invaluable assets. More and more cities recognize that ecological conservation is closely related to residents’ quality of life. To reduce pollution and carbon emissions, some cities are improving public transport, expanding urban green spaces, and promoting waste sorting and green buildings. Green development can not only improve the natural environment but also create new business opportunities. However, achieving long-term goals still requires joint efforts by governments, businesses and the public.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{综合练习 05 · E 基础设施与区域协调}

\noindent {\bfseries 中文题目}\par

\noindent 现代基础设施是区域经济发展的重要支撑。近年来，高速铁路、桥梁、机场和港口的建设不断改善中国各地的交通条件。更加便捷的交通网络缩短了旅行时间，也促进了人员、货物和信息的流动。与此同时，城市建设需要关注公共设施的可及性，确保老人、儿童和残障人士能够更加方便地使用交通服务。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文（先独立完成）}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent Modern infrastructure provides important support for regional economic development. In recent years, the construction of high-speed railways, bridges, airports and ports has continued to improve transport conditions across China. More convenient transport networks have shortened travel times and facilitated the movement of people, goods and information. At the same time, urban development needs to pay attention to accessibility, ensuring that older people, children and people with disabilities can use transport services more easily.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{综合练习 06 · F 传统文化传承与数字传播}

\noindent {\bfseries 中文题目}\par

\noindent 中国传统文化源远流长，书法、剪纸、戏曲等艺术形式具有独特魅力。过去，这些艺术主要通过家庭、学校和民间活动代代相传。如今，数字技术为传统文化传播提供了新的渠道。许多博物馆通过线上展览吸引年轻观众，文化创意产品也逐渐走向国际市场。如何在创新传播方式的同时保留文化特色，已成为文化传承中的重要课题。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文（先独立完成）}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent Traditional Chinese culture has a long and rich history, and art forms such as calligraphy, paper-cutting and traditional opera have a unique appeal. In the past, these arts were mainly passed down through families, schools and community activities. Today, digital technology provides new channels for cultural communication. Many museums attract younger audiences through online exhibitions, and cultural and creative products are gradually entering international markets. How to preserve cultural features while adopting innovative ways of communication has become an important issue in cultural heritage preservation.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{综合练习 07 · G 养老服务与数字社会}

\noindent {\bfseries 中文题目}\par

\noindent 人口老龄化给社会服务带来了新的挑战。为帮助老年人更好地生活，越来越多的社区开设长者食堂，并完善公共场所的无障碍设施。与此同时，手机应用程序和在线服务已经成为日常生活的重要组成部分。有关部门正在努力缩小数字鸿沟，使老年人不仅能够享受数字技术带来的便利，也能获得必要的线下服务。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文（先独立完成）}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent Population aging has created new challenges for social services. To help older people live better lives, more communities are opening dining halls for seniors and improving accessible facilities in public places. Meanwhile, mobile apps and online services have become an important part of daily life. Relevant authorities are working to narrow the digital divide so that older people can not only benefit from digital technology but also access essential offline services.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{综合练习 08 · H 航天科技与科研应用}

\noindent {\bfseries 中文题目}\par

\noindent 中国航天技术的发展为科学研究带来了新的机会。北斗卫星导航系统为交通、农业和公共安全等领域提供定位服务。天宫空间站则为科学家开展空间生命科学、太空医学等研究提供了平台。这些成果不仅体现了科研人员持续创新的能力，也说明科技进步可以通过实际应用改善生产和生活。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文（先独立完成）}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent The development of China’s space technology has created new opportunities for scientific research. The BeiDou Navigation Satellite System provides positioning services for transportation, agriculture and public safety. The Tiangong Space Station offers scientists a platform for research in fields such as space life sciences and space medicine. These achievements not only demonstrate researchers’ capacity for continuous innovation but also show how technological progress can improve production and daily life through practical applications.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{综合练习 09 · I 乡村振兴与城乡融合}

\noindent {\bfseries 中文题目}\par

\noindent 乡村振兴不仅意味着提高农业产量，还包括改善农村公共服务、发展特色产业和保护传统村落。随着农村道路和网络基础设施不断完善，越来越多的农民开始通过电子商务销售当地农产品。一些年轻人也选择返乡创业。这些变化有助于增加农民收入、缩小城乡差距，但各地区仍需根据自身条件选择适当的发展道路。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文（先独立完成）}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent Rural revitalization involves more than increasing agricultural output. It also includes improving public services, developing local industries and preserving traditional villages. As rural roads and internet infrastructure continue to improve, more farmers are selling local agricultural products through e-commerce. Some young people are also returning to their hometowns to start businesses. These changes can increase farmers’ incomes and narrow urban-rural gaps, but different regions still need to choose development paths suited to their local conditions.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{综合练习 10 · J 健康饮食与公共政策}

\noindent {\bfseries 中文题目}\par

\noindent 随着生活水平提高，人们越来越重视饮食健康。如今，消费者不仅关心食物的味道，也更加关注食品安全、营养均衡和质量。有关部门通过开展健康教育、完善食品安全监管等方式，鼓励公众养成合理的饮食习惯。健康饮食有助于预防某些慢性疾病，也为食品产业开发更加多样化的产品提供了新的机会。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文（先独立完成）}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent As living standards improve, people are paying increasing attention to healthy eating. Today, consumers care not only about taste but also about food safety, balanced nutrition and quality. Relevant authorities encourage the public to develop healthier eating habits through health education and stronger food safety regulation. A healthy diet can help prevent certain chronic diseases and create new opportunities for the food industry to develop a wider range of products.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{6\baselineskip}
\section{十二、V2 新增 8 篇跨主题综合模拟}

\begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced,breakable,boxrule=0.35pt,colback=VeryPaleBlue,colframe=AccentBlue!40,left=7pt,right=7pt,top=4pt,bottom=4pt,borderline west={2pt}{0pt}{AccentBlue},before skip=4pt,after skip=6pt]
{\bfseries\color{AccentBlue} 训练方法}\par\vspace{1pt}
\noindent 以下题目均为{\bfseries 原创模拟}，有意将两到三个领域结合。先遮住译文，在 20–25 分钟内独立完成，再检查信息覆盖、语法和搭配。{\bfseries 不是对未来试题的押题保证}。\par


\end{tcolorbox}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{模拟 11·2026 技术融合：无人机 × 智慧农业}

\noindent {\bfseries 请先独立翻译}\par

\noindent 随着农业技术不断进步，无人机正成为现代农田管理的重要工具。农民可以用无人机拍摄高清图像，及时识别病虫害，并根据作物的实际情况精准喷洒农药。与完全依靠人工巡查相比，这种方式能够节省时间，降低作业成本。然而，推广无人机技术还需要加强操作人员培训，完善飞行安全管理，并确保技术能够适应不同地区的农业需求。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent As agricultural technology advances, drones are becoming important tools for modern farm management. Farmers can use them to capture high-definition images, detect crop pests and diseases in time, and spray pesticides precisely according to crop conditions. Compared with relying entirely on manual inspection, this approach can save time and reduce operating costs. However, wider adoption also requires better operator training, sound flight safety management, and technologies suited to the needs of different regions.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{模拟 12·2026 数字乡村：电商 × 物流}

\noindent {\bfseries 请先独立翻译}\par

\noindent 随着网络和物流设施不断向农村延伸，越来越多的农民利用电子商务平台销售当地特色农产品。直播带货使消费者能够直接了解产品，并与卖家实时交流。与此同时，冷链物流的完善减少了新鲜农产品在运输过程中的损耗。这些变化不仅拓宽了农民的销售渠道，也为乡村产业发展创造了新的机会。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent As internet access and logistics facilities expand into rural areas, more farmers are using e-commerce platforms to sell local specialty products. Live-streaming commerce allows consumers to learn about products directly and interact with sellers in real time. Meanwhile, improved cold-chain logistics reduces losses of fresh agricultural products during transport. These changes not only expand sales channels for farmers but also create new opportunities for rural industries.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{模拟 13·2026 医疗发展：远程问诊 × AI}

\noindent {\bfseries 请先独立翻译}\par

\noindent 数字技术正改变医疗服务的提供方式。如今，偏远地区的患者可以通过远程医疗平台与专业医生交流，减少不必要的长途出行。人工智能辅助诊断有助于识别部分疾病的早期症状，电子健康档案则使医生能够更方便地了解患者病史。不过，提高医疗效率的同时，还必须加强个人信息保护，并让不同年龄段的人都能方便地使用这些服务。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent Digital technology is changing the way healthcare services are delivered. Patients in remote areas can now consult specialists through telemedicine platforms, reducing unnecessary long-distance travel. AI-assisted diagnosis can help identify early signs of certain diseases, while electronic health records make it easier for doctors to access patients’ medical histories. However, efforts to improve healthcare efficiency must also protect personal information and ensure that these services are accessible to people of different ages.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{模拟 14·政策发展：新质生产力 × 传统产业}

\noindent {\bfseries 请先独立翻译}\par

\noindent 推动高质量发展不仅需要发展新兴产业，也需要改造提升传统产业。随着人工智能、数字技术和自动化设备得到更广泛应用，一些制造企业正在提高生产效率、改善产品质量。与此同时，政府和企业积极加强科研合作，推动创新成果转化为实际应用。发展新质生产力应当结合各地的产业基础和资源条件，使技术创新更好地服务经济和社会发展。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent Promoting high-quality development requires not only emerging industries but also the upgrading of traditional ones. As artificial intelligence, digital technologies and automated equipment become more widely used, some manufacturers are improving productivity and product quality. Meanwhile, governments and businesses are strengthening research cooperation to turn innovations into practical applications. Developing new quality productive forces should take local industrial strengths and resources into account so that technological innovation can better serve economic and social development.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{模拟 15·生态治理：城市更新 × 低碳生活}

\noindent {\bfseries 请先独立翻译}\par

\noindent 为建设更加宜居的城市，许多地区正在改善公共交通，增加绿地，并改造老旧居民区。这些措施能够提升居民生活质量，也有助于减少能源消耗和环境污染。与此同时，垃圾分类、绿色出行和节约用水逐渐融入人们的日常生活。建设低碳城市并不只是政府的责任，还需要企业和居民共同参与。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent To build more livable cities, many places are improving public transport, expanding green spaces and renovating aging residential communities. These measures can improve residents’ quality of life while helping reduce energy use and environmental pollution. Meanwhile, waste sorting, green travel and water conservation are becoming part of everyday life. Building low-carbon cities is not the responsibility of governments alone; it also requires the participation of businesses and residents.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{模拟 16·未来科技：机器人 × 老龄化}

\noindent {\bfseries 请先独立翻译}\par

\noindent 随着人口老龄化程度加深，社会对养老服务的需求持续增加。近年来，智能设备和服务机器人开始在一些养老机构投入使用，帮助工作人员完成物品运送、健康监测等任务。这些技术有望提高服务效率，但不能完全替代人与人之间的关怀。未来，技术发展应更加重视老年人的实际需要，并通过适当培训帮助他们适应新的服务方式。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent As populations age, demand for eldercare services continues to grow. In recent years, intelligent devices and service robots have begun to be used in some care facilities to help staff deliver supplies and monitor health conditions. These technologies may improve service efficiency, but they cannot fully replace human care. In the future, technological development should pay closer attention to the needs of older adults, while appropriate training can help them adapt to new services.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{模拟 17·传统文化：数字文博 × 年轻群体}

\noindent {\bfseries 请先独立翻译}\par

\noindent 中国丰富的文化遗产为文化创意产业提供了宝贵资源。许多博物馆通过数字展览和线上平台展示馆藏文物，使更多人能够了解历史文化。一些传统工艺也被用于设计具有现代特色的文化产品，受到年轻消费者的欢迎。文化创新并不意味着放弃传统，而是通过新的传播方式，让优秀文化以更生动的形式得到传承。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent China’s rich cultural heritage provides valuable resources for cultural and creative industries. Many museums use digital exhibitions and online platforms to display their collections, allowing more people to learn about history and culture. Some traditional crafts are also incorporated into cultural products with modern features, which appeal to younger consumers. Cultural innovation does not mean abandoning tradition; instead, it can help preserve and pass on valuable cultural traditions through new forms of communication.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{17\baselineskip}
\subsection{模拟 18·国际合作：绿色技术 × 互利发展}

\noindent {\bfseries 请先独立翻译}\par

\noindent 面对气候变化和能源转型等共同挑战，各国需要加强科技交流与合作。绿色能源技术、节能设备和可持续交通方案在许多地区具有广阔的应用前景。通过共享经验、开展联合研究和改善贸易条件，各国可以更有效地利用资源，推动绿色产业发展。不过，国际合作也需要尊重不同国家的发展阶段和实际需求。\par

\Needspace{7\baselineskip}
\noindent\colorbox{HeadBlue}{\strut {\bfseries 展开参考译文}}\par\vspace{1pt}

\noindent In response to shared challenges such as climate change and the energy transition, countries need to strengthen scientific and technological exchanges. Green energy technologies, energy-saving equipment and sustainable transport solutions have broad potential applications in many regions. By sharing experience, conducting joint research and improving trade conditions, countries can use resources more efficiently and promote green industries. However, international cooperation must also respect different stages of development and practical needs.\par

\vspace{4pt}

\Needspace{6\baselineskip}
\section{十三、4 周复习与自测方案}

\specialrule{0.28pt}{0.12pt}{0.12pt}

\vspace{-2pt}

\Needspace{12\baselineskip}
\subsection{一分钟检查清单}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=1.7em,itemsep=1pt,parsep=0pt,topsep=2pt]
\item \noindent \fbox{\rule{0pt}{6pt}\rule{6pt}{0pt}} 每句话都有清楚的主语和谓语；长句太长时是否合理拆分？\par


\item \noindent \fbox{\rule{0pt}{6pt}\rule{6pt}{0pt}} 政策表达是否区分{\bfseries 措施、目的、效果}？\par


\item \noindent \fbox{\rule{0pt}{6pt}\rule{6pt}{0pt}} “通过……使……提高……”是否使用自然的英语动词结构？\par


\item \noindent \fbox{\rule{0pt}{6pt}\rule{6pt}{0pt}} 数字、比例、范围、地名、年份是否准确？\par


\item \noindent \fbox{\rule{0pt}{6pt}\rule{6pt}{0pt}} 对专名没有把握时，是否使用易懂的解释性表达（题目若已给出英文则直接用）？\par


\item \noindent \fbox{\rule{0pt}{6pt}\rule{6pt}{0pt}} 冠词、可数名词、时态、被动结构、介词是否检查？\par


\item \noindent \fbox{\rule{0pt}{6pt}\rule{6pt}{0pt}} 没有额外编造国家政策或技术成效？\par


\end{itemize}

\Needspace{6\baselineskip}
\section{十四、资料来源、验证边界与术语规范}

\noindent 以下链接用于{\bfseries 确定既有命题题材与时代拓展方向}，并不意味着本手册逐词摘录了原题译文。源题参考内容只用于主题核验；表格、例句及练习按翻译教学目的重新编写。\par

\noindent {\bfseries 用户指定真题仓库}\par

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=1.7em,itemsep=1pt,parsep=0pt,topsep=2pt]

\end{itemize}

\noindent {\bfseries 2026 年拓展方向}\par

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=1.7em,itemsep=1pt,parsep=0pt,topsep=2pt]
\item \noindent \href{\detokenize{https://www.miit.gov.cn/xwfb/szyw/art/2026/art_a0f73af7d13b44b080ae9b71903939af.html}}{2026 年政府工作报告（工业和信息化部转载）}：核对高质量发展、数字化、产业升级、绿色发展等方向。\par


\item \noindent \href{\detokenize{https://www.miit.gov.cn/xwfb/szyw/art/2026/art_bb063b402f814c2d829cb53688ee84c0.html}}{中共中央、国务院关于发展新质生产力的意见（工信部网站）}：核对具身智能、脑机接口、低空经济、未来产业等话题。\par


\end{itemize}

\begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced,breakable,boxrule=0.35pt,colback=VeryPaleRose,colframe=WarmRed!40,left=7pt,right=7pt,top=4pt,bottom=4pt,borderline west={2pt}{0pt}{WarmRed},before skip=4pt,after skip=6pt]
{\bfseries\color{WarmRed} 防止两种误解}\par\vspace{1pt}
\noindent （1） 本手册{\bfseries 覆盖题材广}，但不能推导出“能应付未来绝大多数题目”的真实数值概率；（2） 新技术可能出现在政策报告里，却不一定以专业术语考查。实际备考更要练习如何用普通词汇准确表达新技术的{\bfseries 应用、价值与影响}。\par


\end{tcolorbox}

\begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced,breakable,boxrule=0.35pt,colback=VeryPaleBlue,colframe=AccentBlue!40,left=7pt,right=7pt,top=4pt,bottom=4pt,borderline west={2pt}{0pt}{AccentBlue},before skip=4pt,after skip=6pt]
{\bfseries\color{AccentBlue} 完成本手册的目标}\par\vspace{1pt}
\noindent 不是能够背诵所有具体领域的术语，而是面对一个以前未接触的主题时，也能把中文材料的{\bfseries 主干事实、行为过程、因果关系与社会意义}准确地译成自然英语。\par


\end{tcolorbox}
